contravention of a provision of Division 5 of Part 7 by a company
engaged in the business of assembling or altering vehicles, it is a
defence for the company to establish that the contravention occurred as
a result of work previously done on a vehicle by another company engaged
in the manufacture of the vehicle.

## [PART 11]{.HLabel1}[Miscellaneous Matters]{.HTitleText1} {#h-67022 .Part}

### [Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67023 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for confidentiality

- **[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who provides information to the Minister under this Act, or to
  a board of review in respect of a notice of objection filed under this
  Act, may submit with the information a request that it be treated as
  confidential.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of request

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A request for confidentiality shall be submitted,
  with reasons taking into account the criteria set out in paragraphs
  20(1)(a) to (d) of the [Access to Information Act](/eng/acts/A-1), in
  writing and contain any supplementary information that may be
  prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of requests

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall review a statistically valid
  representative sample of requests granted under subsection (1) and
  determine whether, in respect of each of those requests, the person
  who made the request demonstrated that it concerned any of the
  following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} trade secrets of any person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} financial, commercial, scientific or technical
    information that is confidential information and that is treated
    consistently in a confidential manner by any person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information the disclosure of which could
    reasonably be expected to result in material financial loss or gain
    to, or could reasonably be expected to prejudice the competitive
    position of, any person; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} information the disclosure of which could
    reasonably be expected to interfere with contractual or other
    negotiations of any person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requests deemed not to have been made

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister determines that the person who made
  the request did not demonstrate that the request, in whole or in part,
  concerned information described in any of paragraphs (3)(a) to (d),
  then, for the purpose of any part of the request that does not concern
  such information, the request is deemed not to have been made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report --- number of requests

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall include in the annual report
  required under section 342 the number of requests made under
  subsection (1), the number of requests reviewed, the number of
  requests that, in whole or in part, were deemed not to have been made
  and a summary of the information disclosed under sections 315 to
  317.2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by order, designate any person or
  class of persons to exercise the powers and perform the duties and
  functions set out in this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 313
- [2023, c. 12, s. 50](#1403073-1404652){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403073-1404652.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-313-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 313"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition of disclosure

**[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** The Minister shall not
disclose any information in respect of which a request for
confidentiality has been made under section 313, except in accordance
with any of sections 315 to 317.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 314
- [2023, c. 12, s. 51](#1403078-1404657){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403078-1404657.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-314-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 314"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure by Minister for public
interest

- **[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may disclose information, other than information in respect
  of which section 318 applies, where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the disclosure is in the interest of public health,
    public safety or the protection of the environment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the public interest in the disclosure clearly
    outweighs in importance

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any material financial loss or prejudice to the
      competitive position of the person who provided the information or
      on whose behalf it was provided, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any damage to the privacy, reputation or human
      dignity of any individual that may result from the disclosure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of disclosure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), at least 24 hours before
  disclosing any information under subsection (1), the Minister shall
  give notice of the proposed disclosure to the person who provided the
  information or on whose behalf it was provided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notice under subsection (2)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not required where the person to whom it is to
    be given cannot be found after reasonable efforts have been made to
    do so; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may be given later than the time required by that
    subsection where an emergency exists.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain purposes for which information
may be disclosed

- **[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Information may be disclosed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with the written consent of the person who provided
    it or on whose behalf it was provided;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as may be necessary for the purposes of the
    administration or enforcement of this Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} under an agreement or arrangement between the
    Government of Canada or any of its institutions and any other
    government in Canada, the government of a foreign state or an
    international organization or any of its institutions, or between
    the Minister and any other minister of the Crown in right of Canada
    or a board or agency of the Government of Canada, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the purpose of the agreement or arrangement is
      the administration or enforcement of a law, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the government, international organization,
      institution, other minister, board or agency undertakes to keep
      the information confidential;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} under an agreement or arrangement between the
    Government of Canada and the government of a foreign state or an
    international organization, where the government or organization
    undertakes to keep the information confidential; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} to a physician or prescribed medical professional
    who requests the information for the purpose of making a medical
    diagnosis of, or rendering medical treatment to, a person in an
    emergency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure by physician, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A physician or prescribed medical professional to
  whom information is disclosed under paragraph (1)(e) shall not
  disclose the information except as may be necessary for the purposes
  referred to in that paragraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of personal information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Personal information as defined in section 3 of the
  [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21) may not be disclosed under paragraph
  (1)(b) or (c) unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the disclosure is in the interest of public health,
    public safety or the protection of the environment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the public interest in the disclosure clearly
    outweighs in importance any damage to the privacy, reputation or
    human dignity of any individual that may result from the disclosure.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 316
- [2023, c. 12, s. 52](#1403080-1404662){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403080-1404662.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-316-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 316"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure by Minister

- **[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may disclose information in respect of which a request for
  confidentiality has been made under section 313 where the Minister
  determines that the disclosure would not be prohibited under section
  20 of the [Access to Information Act](/eng/acts/A-1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of certain provisions of
  [Access to Information Act](/eng/acts/A-1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister intends to disclose information
  under subsection (1), sections 27, 28 and 44 of the [Access to
  Information Act](/eng/acts/A-1) apply, with any modifications that the
  circumstances require, and, for that purpose, that information is
  deemed to be contained in a record that the Minister intends to
  disclose and any reference in those sections to the person who
  requested access shall be disregarded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection from civil proceeding or
  prosecution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other Act of Parliament, no civil or
  criminal proceedings lie against the Minister, or against any person
  acting on behalf of or under the direction of the Minister, and no
  proceedings lie against the Crown for the disclosure in good faith of
  any information under this Act, for any consequences that flow from
  that disclosure or for the failure to give any notice required under
  section 27 or any other provision of the [Access to Information
  Act](/eng/acts/A-1) if reasonable care is taken to give the required
  notice.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure --- substance

- **[[317.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may disclose the
  explicit chemical or biological name of a substance in respect of
  which a request for confidentiality has been made if the Minister
  takes or has taken any of the following measures:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to permit, under paragraph 84(1)(a), the
    manufacture or importation of the substance, subject to any
    conditions that are specified;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to prohibit the manufacture or importation of the
    substance under paragraph 84(1)(b);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to publish, in respect of the substance, a notice
    under subsection 85(1) or (4); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to amend the Domestic Substances List in respect of
    the substance under subsection 87(3) or (4.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure --- living organism

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may disclose the explicit biological
  name of a living organism in respect of which a request for
  confidentiality has been made if the Minister takes or has taken any
  of the following measures:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to permit, under paragraph 109(1)(a), the
    manufacture or importation of the living organism, subject to any
    conditions that are specified;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to prohibit the manufacture or importation of the
    living organism under paragraph 109(1)(b);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to publish, in respect of the living organism, a
    notice under subsection 110(1) or (4); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to amend the Domestic Substances List in respect of
    the living organism under subsection 112(3) or (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure --- Ministers'
  recommendation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall disclose the explicit chemical or
  biological name of a substance or the explicit biological name of a
  living organism in respect of which a request for confidentiality has
  been made if the Ministers recommend or have recommended to the
  Governor in Council the addition of the substance or the living
  organism to Part 1 or 2 of the list of toxic substances in Schedule 1
  under subsection 90(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 12, s. 53](#1403085-1404679){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403085-1404679.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure after expiry

- **[[317.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may disclose the
  explicit chemical or biological name of a substance or the explicit
  biological name of a living organism after the expiry of a period of
  10 years after the day on which a request for confidentiality under
  section 313 is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication --- notice of intent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall publish a notice in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) of the Minister's intention to
  disclose the explicit chemical or biological name under subsection (1)
  at least 60 days before the proposed disclosure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comments

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Within 60 days after the publication of the notice,
  the person who made the request for confidentiality or the person's
  successor or assign may file with the Minister comments respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whether the public interest in the disclosure of
    the name clearly outweighs in importance

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any material financial loss or prejudice to the
      competitive position of the person or the person's successor or
      assign, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any damage to the privacy, reputation or human
      dignity of any person that may result from the disclosure of that
      name; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether the disclosure is prohibited under section
    20 of the [Access to Information Act](/eng/acts/A-1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of disclosure

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} At least 24 hours before disclosing the name under
  subsection (1), the Minister shall give notice of the proposed
  disclosure to the person who made the request for confidentiality or
  the person's successor or assign. The notice is not required if the
  person to whom it is to be given cannot be found after reasonable
  efforts have been made to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emergency

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If an emergency exists, subsections (2) and (3) do
  not apply and the notice of disclosure required in subsection (4) may
  be given later than the time provided for in that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 12, s. 53](#1403085-1404696){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403085-1404696.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to Parliament

**[[317.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317.3 .sectionLabel}** The Minister shall
include in the annual report required by section 342 a report respecting
the explicit chemical or biological names of substances and the explicit
biological names of living organisms disclosed under section 317.1 or
317.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 12, s. 53](#1403085-1404700){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403085-1404700.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Hazardous Materials Information Review
Act](/eng/acts/H-2.7)

**[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** Except as provided in
sections 316 and 317, information in respect of which a request for
confidentiality has been made under section 313 shall not be disclosed
where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a claim for exemption has been filed in respect of
  the information under section 11 of the [Hazardous Materials
  Information Review Act](/eng/acts/H-2.7);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information is exempt under section 19 of that
  Act from the requirement in respect of which the exemption is claimed;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person claiming the exemption has disclosed the
  contents of the claim to the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations prescribing

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} information that shall accompany a request made under
  section 313; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} medical professionals for the purposes of paragraph
  316(1)(e).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-disclosure by Minister of National
Defence

**[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** Despite any other
provision of this Act, the Minister of National Defence may refuse to
disclose under this Part any information the disclosure of which could
reasonably be expected to be injurious to the defence or security of
Canada or of a state allied or associated with Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security requirements for disclosure

**[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321 .sectionLabel}** Any person, except an
enforcement officer or analyst, who receives, obtains or has access to
information under this Act shall comply with any security requirements
applicable to, and take any oath of secrecy required to be taken by,
persons who normally have access to and use of the information.

### [Economic Instruments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67080 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines, programs and other measures

**[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
establish guidelines, programs and other measures for the development
and use of economic instruments and market-based approaches to further
the purposes of this Act, respecting systems relating to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deposits and refunds; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} compliance units.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 322
- [2023, c. 26, s. 619](#1411198-1414957){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1411198-1414957.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-322-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 322"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation

- **[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  carrying out the responsibilities conferred by section 322, the
  Minister shall offer to consult with the government of a province and
  the members of the Committee who are representatives of aboriginal
  governments and may consult with a government department or agency,
  aboriginal people, representatives of industry and labour and
  municipal authorities or with persons interested in quality of the
  environment or the preservation and improvement of public health.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} At any time after the 60th day following the day on
  which the Minister offers to consult in accordance with subsection
  (1), the Minister may act under section 322 if the offer to consult is
  not accepted by the government of a province or members of the
  Committee who are representatives of aboriginal governments.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of guidelines and programs

**[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324 .sectionLabel}** The Minister shall
publish any guidelines, programs and other measures established under
section 322, or shall give notice of their availability, in the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and in any other manner that the
Minister considers appropriate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations for systems relating to
deposits and refunds

**[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, in the exercise of a regulation-making power under section 93, 118
or 209, make regulations respecting systems relating to deposits and
refunds, including regulations providing for, or imposing requirements
respecting,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deposits, including the amount of any deposit and the
  substance, product containing a substance or activity in relation to
  which a deposit is required, the conditions for the use of a deposit
  and the conditions for and manner of paying a deposit;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period during which a deposit may be held;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} refunds, including the amount of any refund and the
  substance, product containing a substance or activity in relation to
  which a refund may be granted and the conditions for and manner of
  paying a refund;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the establishment of a fund for deposits, and the
  operation, management and administration of the fund;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the designation of a person to administer the fund
  for deposits and the conditions for the designation;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} reports and forms related to deposits, refunds and
  the fund for deposits;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the maintenance of books and records for the
  administration of any regulation made under this section; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the forfeiture of deposits, including unclaimed
  deposits and the conditions under which and the circumstances in which
  deposits may be forfeited.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 325
- 2017, c. 26, s. 63(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-325-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 325"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations for compliance units systems

**[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, in the exercise of a regulation-making power under section 93, 118,
140, 167, 177 or 209, make regulations respecting systems relating to
compliance units, including regulations providing for, or imposing
requirements respecting,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the substance, product containing a substance or
  quantity or concentration of the substance that is released or
  activity in relation to which the system is established;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the methods and procedures for conducting sampling,
  analyses, tests, measurements or monitoring under the system;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the description and nature of a compliance unit,
  including allowances, credits or coupons;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the baselines to be used for comparison or control
  purposes in relation to the system and the maximum limits applicable
  to the system and the manner of determining those baselines and
  maximum limits;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the conditions related to the creation, distribution,
  trade, sale, use, variation or cancellation of a compliance unit;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the creation, operation and management of a public
  registry related to the system;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the conditions for the use of and participation in
  the system, including environmental or temporal limits;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} reports and forms related to the system; and

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the maintenance of books and records for the
  administration of any regulation made under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 326
- [2023, c. 26, s. 620](#1411200-1415007){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1411200-1415007.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-326-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 326"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ministerial orders

**[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** Despite any regulation
made under section 326, the Minister may issue an order setting
conditions in respect of the trading or suspend or cancel trading of
compliance units or invalidate any trade of compliance units if the
Ministers are of the opinion that the trade or use of a compliance unit

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has or may have an immediate or long-term harmful
  effect on the environment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} constitutes or may constitute a danger to the
  environment on which human life depends; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} constitutes or may constitute a danger in Canada to
  human life or health.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 327
- [2023, c. 26, s. 621](#1411211-1415011){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1411211-1415011.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-327-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 327"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Environmental Economic Instruments Fund

- **[[327.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There is established an account in
  the accounts of Canada to be called the Environmental Economic
  Instruments Fund, consisting of a sub-account for each funding program
  that meets the following conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its purpose is provided for in a regulation made
    under section 326 and any of sections 93, 118, 140, 167, 177 and
    209; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is under the responsibility of the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amounts paid into C.R.F.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amounts received by His Majesty in right of
  Canada as contributions to a funding program referred to in
  subsection (1) are to be paid into the Consolidated Revenue Fund and
  credited to the sub-account for that program.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Finance shall cause to be credited to
  each sub-account an amount representing interest, calculated in the
  manner and at the rate determined by the Governor in Council on the
  recommendation of that Minister, on the amount standing to the credit
  of that sub-account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amounts paid out of C.R.F.

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On the Minister's request, one or more amounts not
  exceeding in total the amount standing in the sub-account for a
  funding program referred to in subsection (1), including interest, may
  be paid out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund for the purpose for which
  that funding program was established. The amount of all such payments
  is to be charged to that sub-account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administration of Fund

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister is responsible for the administration of
  the Environmental Economic Instruments Fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Amounts credited to a sub-account for a funding
  program referred to in subsection (1) may be used for the
  administration of that sub-account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by order, designate any person or
  class of persons to exercise the powers and perform the duties and
  functions set out in subsections (4) and (5).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 622](#1411213-1415030){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1411213-1415030.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Regulations Respecting Fees and Charges]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67120 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Services and facilities

- **[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  Minister may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the fees or a scale of fees or the
    manner of determining the fees to be paid for a service, the use of
    a facility or any right, privilege, process or approval;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the persons or classes of persons by
    whom or on whose behalf the fees are to be paid and requiring the
    fees to be paid by those persons or classes of persons;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} exempting any person or class of persons from the
    requirement to pay any of those fees; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} generally, in respect of any condition or any other
    matter in relation to the payment of fees for a service, facility,
    right, privilege, process or approval referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appropriate Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), where the Minister
  or the Minister of Health or both Ministers have responsibility for a
  service, facility, right, privilege, process or approval referred to
  in paragraph (1)(a), that Minister or both Ministers, as the case may
  be, have the power to make the regulations under subsection (1) in
  relation to that service, facility, right, privilege, process or
  approval.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount not to exceed cost

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Fees for a service or the use of a facility that are
  prescribed by or under regulations made under subsection (1) may not
  exceed the cost to Her Majesty in right of Canada of providing the
  service or the use of the facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Fees for processes or approvals that are prescribed
  by or under regulations made under subsection (1) shall in the
  aggregate not exceed an amount sufficient to compensate Her Majesty in
  right of Canada for any reasonable costs incurred by Her Majesty for
  the purpose of providing the processes or approvals.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation

**[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** The Minister making a
regulation under section 328 shall do so after consulting with any
persons or organizations that the Minister considers to be interested in
the matter.

### [General Regulation-making Powers and Exemptions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67136 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exercise of regulation-making power

- **[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, in the exercise of any regulation-making
  power under this Act in respect of a substance, prescribe

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the minimum, average or maximum quantity or
    concentration of the substance; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the method of determining such a quantity or
    concentration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a regulation made under this
  Act incorporating by reference a standard, specification, guideline,
  method, procedure or practice may incorporate the standard,
  specification, guideline, method, procedure or practice as amended
  from time to time.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2023, c. 12, s. 54\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2023, c. 12, s. 54\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variation

  [(3.2)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under section 93, 140, 145, 167,
  177 or 326 may distinguish among any class of persons, works,
  undertakings, activities or substances, including fuels, that they may
  establish on the basis of any factor, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} quantities of releases;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} production capacity;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} technology or techniques used or manufacturing
    process;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} feedstocks used;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of works or undertakings, the date
    their operation commenced or the date on which any major alterations
    are completed;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the substance or fuel's source;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the substance or fuel's commercial designation;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the substance or fuel's physical or chemical
    properties; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the substance or fuel's conditions of use or place
    or time of year of use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation of Part 7

  [(3.3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in Part 7 is to be construed so as to
  prevent the making of regulations under Part 5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of service

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may make regulations respecting the
  manner of providing or serving orders, copies of orders, notices or
  other documents that are to be provided under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 330
- 2008, c. 31, s. 5
- [2023, c. 12, s. 54](#1403087-1404703){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403087-1404703.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-330-20090928.html "Link to previous version of section 330"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption from [Statutory Instruments
Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

**[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** An interim order made
under section 94, 163, 173, 183 or 200.1

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is exempt from the application of sections 3, 5 and
  11 of the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) within 23 days after it is
  approved by the Governor in Council.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 331
- 2004, c. 15, s. 30
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-331-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 331"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Prepublication Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67171 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of proposed orders and
regulations

- **[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister shall publish in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) a copy of every order or
  regulation proposed to be made by the Minister or the Governor in
  Council under this Act, except a list, or an amendment to a list,
  referred to in any of sections 66 to 66.2, 87, 105 to 105.2 and 112 or
  an interim order made under section 94, 163, 173, 183 or 200.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of objection

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person may file with the Minister comments, or a
  notice of objection requesting that a board of review be established
  under section 333 and stating the reasons for the objection, within 60
  days after the publication of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a proposed order or regulation referred to in
    subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a proposed instrument respecting preventive or
    control actions in relation to a substance that is required by
    section 91 to be published in the [Canada
    Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement referred to in subparagraph
    91(1)(a)(ii) or (b)(ii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single publication required

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No order, regulation or instrument need be published
  more than once under subsection (1), whether or not it is altered
  after publication.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 332
- 2004, c. 15, s. 31
- [2023, c. 12, s. 55](#1403088-1404710){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403088-1404710.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-332-20041001.html "Link to previous version of section 332"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Board of Review Proceedings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67181 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment of board of review

- **[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person files a notice of objection under subsection 332(2) in respect
  of a decision or a proposed order, regulation or instrument made by
  the Governor in Council, a decision or a proposed order or instrument
  made by either or both Ministers or a statement referred to in
  subparagraph 91(1)(a)(ii) or (b)(ii), the Minister or the Ministers
  may establish a board of review to inquire into the nature and extent
  of the danger posed by the substance in respect of which the decision
  or statement is made or the order, regulation or instrument is
  proposed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment of board of review

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person files a notice of objection under
  subsection 9(3) or 10(5) in respect of an agreement or a term or
  condition of the agreement, the Minister may establish a board of
  review to inquire into the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory review for international air
  and water

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a person or government files with the Minister
  a notice of objection under subsection 332(2) with respect to
  regulations proposed to be made under section 167 or 177 within the
  time specified in that subsection, the Minister shall establish a
  board of review to inquire into the nature and extent of the danger
  posed by the release into the air or water of the substance in respect
  of which the regulations are proposed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory reviews for certain
  regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a person files with the Minister a notice of
  objection under subsection 332(2) with respect to regulations proposed
  to be made under Part 9 or section 118 within the time specified in
  that subsection, the Minister shall establish a board of review to
  inquire into the matter raised by the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review for permits

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a person files with the Minister a notice of
  objection under section 134 within the time specified in that section,
  the Minister may establish a board of review to inquire into the
  matter raised by the notice.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2023, c. 12, s. 56\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 333
- [2023, c. 12, s. 56](#1403092-1404715){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403092-1404715.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-333-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 333"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of members of board

- **[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board of review shall consist of not fewer than three members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Knowledge requirement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person is not eligible to be appointed as a member
  of a board of review unless the person is knowledgeable about the
  Canadian environment, environmental and human health or traditional
  aboriginal ecological knowledge.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appearance before board

**[[335]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-335 .sectionLabel}** A board of review shall
give any person or government a reasonable opportunity, consistent with
the rules of procedural fairness and natural justice, of appearing
before it, presenting evidence and making representations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal of notice of objection

**[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** Where a notice of
objection referred to in section 333 is withdrawn by the person who
filed it and no other notice of objection is filed in respect of the
same matter, the Minister or Ministers may dissolve a board of review
established in respect of the notice of objection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of board

**[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of an
inquiry under this Act, a board of review has all the powers of a person
appointed as a commissioner under Part I of the [Inquiries
Act](/eng/acts/I-11).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs

- **[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board of review may award costs of, and incidental to, any proceedings
  before it on a final or interim basis and those costs may be fixed at
  a sum certain or may be taxed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A board of review may, in accordance with the rules,
  direct by whom and to whom any costs are to be paid and by whom they
  are to be taxed and allowed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absent member of board

**[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339 .sectionLabel}** Where a member of a
board of review is absent or unable to proceed with or complete an
inquiry, the remaining members of the board may, if there is still a
quorum, proceed with or complete the inquiry.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

- **[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} As
  soon as possible after the conclusion of an inquiry, a board of review
  shall submit a report to the Minister or the Ministers who established
  the board, together with its recommendations and the evidence that was
  presented to it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The report of the board of review shall be made
  public immediately after its receipt by the Minister or Ministers,
  subject to section 314 or the conditions of any other Act of
  Parliament.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules for boards of review

**[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341 .sectionLabel}** The Minister or the
Ministers may, with respect to boards of review that the Minister or the
Ministers establish, make rules of either particular or general
application

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for regulating the proceedings of the boards;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for regulating the conduct of hearings, including the
  presentation of evidence, the making of representations, the holding
  of hearings, the length of hearings and the date on or before which
  the boards shall report;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fixing the remuneration of board members and the
  travel and living expenses to which they are entitled;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the criteria to be considered by boards
  in directing interim or final costs to be paid, taxed and allowed and
  prescribing a scale under which costs are to be taxed; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} generally, for regulating the conduct and work of the
  boards.

### [Report to Parliament]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67228 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual report

- **[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister shall, as soon as possible after the end of each fiscal year,
  prepare and cause to be laid before each House of Parliament a report
  on the administration and enforcement of this Act for that year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Research

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall include in the annual report a
  report on the research conducted under the authority of this Act
  during the fiscal year being reported.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report --- aboriginal peoples

**[[342.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342.1 .sectionLabel}** The Minister shall
include in the annual report required by section 342 information related
to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} consultations with aboriginal peoples and aboriginal
  governments, including a summary of the key issues raised, in relation
  to matters under this Act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the administration of this Act in respect of
  aboriginal peoples and aboriginal governments, including the measures
  taken to advance reconciliation as reflected in section 35 of the
  [Constitution Act, 1982](/eng/Const/) and in the [United Nations
  Declaration on the Rights of Indigenous Peoples Act](/eng/acts/U-2.2);
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the key findings or recommendations of any report
  made under an Act of Parliament in respect of the administration of
  this Act and aboriginal peoples and aboriginal governments.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 12, s. 57](#1403096-1404722){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1403096-1404722.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent review of Act

- **[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  administration of this Act shall, every five years after the coming
  into force of this Act, stand referred to such committee of the House
  of Commons, of the Senate or of both Houses of Parliament as may be
  designated or established for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review and report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The committee designated or established for the
  purpose of subsection (1) shall, as soon as practicable, undertake a
  comprehensive review of the provisions and operation of this Act and
  shall, within one year after the review is undertaken or within such
  further time as the House of Commons, the Senate or both Houses of
  Parliament, as the case may be, may authorize, submit a report to
  Parliament thereon, including a statement of any changes to this Act
  or its administration that the committee would recommend.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 33, s. 343
- 2017, c. 26, s. 31(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-343-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 343"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 12]{.HLabel1}[Consequential Amendments, Repeal, Transitional Provision and Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-67241 .Part}

### [Consequential Amendments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67242 .Subheading}

**[[344 to 354]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344_to_354
.sectionLabel}** \[Amendments\]

### [Repeal]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67244 .Subheading}

**[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355 .sectionLabel}** \[Repeal\]

### [Transitional]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67246 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[355.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), any
  regulation that was

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made under the Act mentioned in section 355, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in force immediately before the day on which this
    Act is assented to

  is deemed to have been made under this Act, and continues in force,
  subject to being amended or repealed under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where inconsistent with this Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a regulation continued in force by subsection (1)
  is not consistent with this Act at the end of the two-year period that
  starts on the day on which this Act is assented to, that regulation
  ceases to be in force at the end of that period.

### [Coming into Force]{.HTitleText2} {#h-67255 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coming into force

**[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#C-15.31_en_1){#C-15.31_en_1-ID0EBCA}[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356
.sectionLabel}** This Act or any of its provisions comes into force on a
day or days to be fixed by order of the Governor in Council.

  and sections 107 to 233, 242, 256 to 331 and 342 to 355.1 in force

<div class="listItemText2">
2-Propenamide, which has the molecular formula
C<sub>3</sub>H<sub>5</sub>NO
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>100</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Ethanol, 2-chloro-, phosphate (3:1), which has the molecular formula
C<sub>6</sub>H<sub>12</sub>Cl<sub>3</sub>O<sub>4</sub>P
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>101</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Tetrabutyltins, which have the molecular formula
(C<sub>4</sub>H<sub>9</sub>)<sub>4</sub>Sn
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>102</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Benzene, (chloromethyl)-, which has the molecular formula
C<sub>7</sub>H<sub>7</sub>Cl
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>103</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Propane, 2-nitro-, which has the molecular formula
C<sub>3</sub>H<sub>7</sub>NO<sub>2</sub>
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>104</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Benzene, 1-methyl-2-nitro-, which has the molecular formula
C<sub>7</sub>H<sub>7</sub>NO<sub>2</sub>
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>105</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Methylium, [4-(dimethylamino)phenyl]bis[4-(ethylamino)3-methylphenyl]-,
acetate, which has the molecular formula
C<sub>27</sub>H<sub>34</sub>N<sub>3</sub>.C<sub>2</sub>H<sub>3</sub>O<sub>2</sub>
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>106</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Benzene, 1,2-dimethoxy-4-(2-propenyl)-, which has the molecular formula
C<sub>11</sub>H<sub>14</sub>O<sub>2</sub>
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>107</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Vanadium pentoxide, which has the molecular formula
V<sub>2</sub>O<sub>5</sub>
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>108</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Oxirane,
2,2′,2′′,2′′′-[1,2-ethanediylidenetetrakis(4,1-phenyleneoxymethylene)]tetrakis-,
which has the molecular formula
C<sub>38</sub>H<sub>38</sub>O<sub>8</sub>
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>109</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Bromic acid, potassium salt, which has the molecular formula
KBrO<sub>3</sub>
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>110</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Hydrazine, which has the molecular formula N<sub>2</sub>H<sub>4</sub>
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>111</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Quinoline, which has the molecular formula C<sub>9</sub>H<sub>7</sub>N
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>112</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Perfluorooctanoic acid, which has the molecular formula
C<sub>7</sub>F<sub>15</sub>CO<sub>2</sub>H, and its salts
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>113</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Compounds that consist of a perfluorinated alkyl group that has the
molecular formula C<sub>n</sub>F<sub>2n+1</sub> in which n is equal to 7
or 8 and that is directly bonded to any chemical moiety other than a
fluorine, chlorine or bromine atom
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>114</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Perfluorocarboxylic acids that have the molecular formula
C<sub>n</sub>F<sub>2n+1</sub>CO<sub>2</sub>H in which 8≤n≤20 and their
salts
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>115</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Compounds that consist of a perfluorinated alkyl group that has the
molecular formula C<sub>n</sub>F<sub>2n+1</sub> in which 8≤n≤ 20 and
that is directly bonded to any chemical moiety other than a fluorine,
chlorine or bromine atom
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>116</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Plastic microbeads that are less than or equal to 5 mm in size
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>117</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
The following petroleum and refinery gases:
</div>
</div>
<figure>
<ul>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(a)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
tail gas (petroleum), catalytic polymerized naphtha fractionation
stabilizer (a complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the
fractionation stabilization products that result from the polymerization
of naphtha — consisting predominantly of hydrocarbons having carbon
numbers in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(b)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
fuel gases (a combination of light gases consisting predominantly of
hydrogen or low molecular weight hydrocarbons or both);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(c)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
hydrocarbons, C<sub>2</sub>-C<sub>4</sub>, C<sub>3</sub>-rich (a complex
combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from a treating process to remove
sulphur and other acidic compounds — consisting of hydrocarbons having
carbon numbers in the range of C<sub>2</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>,
predominantly propane and propene);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(d)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), butane splitter overhead (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation of the butane stream —
consisting of aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly
in the range of C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(e)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), catalytic cracked gas oil depropanizer bottom,
C<sub>4</sub>-rich acid-free (a complex combination of hydrocarbons —
obtained from the fractionation of catalytic cracked gas oil hydrocarbon
stream and treated to remove hydrogen sulfide and other acidic
components — consisting of hydrocarbons having carbon numbers in the
range of C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>, predominantly
C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(f)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), catalytic cracked naphtha debutanizer bottom,
C<sub>3</sub>-C<sub>5</sub>-rich (a complex combination of hydrocarbons
— obtained from the stabilization of catalytic cracked naphtha —
consisting of aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly
in the range of C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(g)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), catalytic cracked naphtha depropanizer overhead,
C<sub>3</sub>-rich acid-free (a complex combination of hydrocarbons —
obtained from the fractionation of catalytic cracked hydrocarbons and
treated to remove acidic impurities — consisting of hydrocarbons having
carbon numbers in the range of C<sub>2</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>,
predominantly C<sub>3</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(h)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), catalytic cracker, C<sub>1</sub>-C<sub>5</sub>-rich
(a complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation
of products that result from a catalytic cracking process — consisting
of aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers in the range of
C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>6</sub>, predominantly C<sub>1</sub> through
C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(i)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), catalytic polymerized naphtha stabilizer overhead,
C<sub>2</sub>-C<sub>4</sub>-rich (a complex combination of hydrocarbons
— obtained from the fractionation stabilization of catalytic polymerized
naphtha — consisting of aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers in
the range of C<sub>2</sub> through C<sub>6</sub>, predominantly
C<sub>2</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(j)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), catalytic reformed naphtha stripper overhead (a
complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the stabilization of
catalytic reformed naphtha — consisting of hydrogen and saturated
aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the range
of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(k)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), deethanizer overhead (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation of the gas and gasoline
fractions that result from a catalytic cracking process — consisting
predominantly of ethane and ethene);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(l)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), deisobutanizer tower overhead (a complex combination
of hydrocarbons — obtained from the atmospheric distillation of a
butane-butene stream — consisting of aliphatic hydrocarbons having
carbon numbers predominantly in the range of C<sub>3</sub> through
C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(m)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), gas concentration reabsorber distillation (a complex
combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation of products
from combined gas streams in a gas concentration reabsorber — consisting
predominantly of hydrogen, carbon monoxide, carbon dioxide, nitrogen,
hydrogen sulfide and hydrocarbons having carbon numbers in the range of
C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>3</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(n)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), hydrogen-rich (a complex combination — separated as a
gas from hydrocarbon gases by chilling — consisting predominantly of
hydrogen with small amounts of carbon monoxide, nitrogen, methane and
C<sub>2</sub> hydrocarbons);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(o)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), recycle, hydrogen-rich (a complex combination —
obtained from recycled reactor gases — consisting predominantly of
hydrogen with small amounts of carbon monoxide, carbon dioxide,
nitrogen, hydrogen sulfide and saturated aliphatic hydrocarbons having
carbon numbers in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(p)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), reformer make-up, hydrogen-rich (a complex
combination — obtained from the reformers — consisting predominantly of
hydrogen with small amounts of carbon monoxide and aliphatic
hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of
C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(q)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), thermal cracking distillation (a complex combination
— obtained from the distillation of products that result from a thermal
cracking process — consisting of hydrogen, hydrogen sulfide, carbon
monoxide, carbon dioxide and hydrocarbons having carbon numbers
predominantly in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>6</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(r)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
tail gas (petroleum), catalytic cracker refractionation absorber (a
complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the refractionation
of products that result from a catalytic cracking process — consisting
of hydrogen and hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the
range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>3</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(s)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
tail gas (petroleum), cracked distillate hydrotreater separator (a
complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained by treating cracked
distillates with hydrogen in the presence of a catalyst — consisting of
hydrogen and saturated aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers
predominantly in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(t)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
tail gas (petroleum), saturate gas plant mixed stream,
C<sub>4</sub>-rich (a complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained
from the fractionation stabilization of straight-run naphtha,
distillation tail gas and catalytic reformed naphtha stabilizer tail gas
— consisting of hydrocarbons having carbon numbers in the range of
C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>6</sub>, predominantly butane and
isobutane);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(u)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
tail gas (petroleum), vacuum residue thermal cracker (a complex
combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the thermal cracking of
vacuum residues — consisting of hydrocarbons having carbon numbers
predominantly in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(v)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
hydrocarbons, C<sub>3</sub>-C<sub>4</sub>-rich, petroleum distillates (a
complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation and
condensation of crude oil — consisting of hydrocarbons having carbon
numbers in the range of C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>,
predominantly C<sub>3</sub> and C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(w)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), hydrocracking depropanizer off, hydrocarbon-rich (a
complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation of
products that result from a hydrocracking process — consisting
predominantly of hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the
range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(x)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), light straight-run naphtha stabilizer off (a complex
combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the stabilization of light
straight-run naphtha — consisting of saturated aliphatic hydrocarbons
having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of C<sub>2</sub>
through C<sub>6</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(y)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), reformer effluent high-pressure flash drum off (a
complex combination — obtained from the high-pressure flashing of the
effluent from the reforming reactor — consisting predominantly of
hydrogen with small amounts of methane, ethane and propane);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
hydrocarbons, C<sub>1</sub>-C<sub>4</sub> (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained from thermal cracking and absorber operations
and from the distillation of crude oil — consisting of hydrocarbons
having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of C<sub>1</sub>
through C<sub>4</sub> and boiling in the range of approximately -164°C
to -0.5°C);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.1)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
hydrocarbons, C<sub>1</sub>-C<sub>4</sub>, sweetened (a complex
combination of hydrocarbons — obtained by subjecting hydrocarbon gases
to a sweetening process to convert mercaptans or to remove acidic
impurities — consisting of hydrocarbons having carbon numbers
predominantly in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>4</sub> and
boiling in the range of approximately -164°C to -0.5°C);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.2)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
hydrocarbons, C<sub>1</sub>-C<sub>3</sub> (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of
C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>3</sub> and boiling in the range of
approximately -164°C to -42°C);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.3)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), C<sub>1</sub>-C<sub>5</sub>, wet (a complex
combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation of crude
oil or the cracking of tower gas oil or both — consisting of
hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of
C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.4)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), secondary absorber off, fluidized catalytic cracker
overhead fractionater (a complex combination — obtained from the
fractionation of the overhead products that result from a catalytic
cracking process in the fluidized catalytic cracker — consisting of
hydrogen, nitrogen and hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly
in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>3</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.5)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), alkylation feed (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained from the catalytic cracking of gas oil —
consisting of hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the
range of C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.6)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
petroleum products, refinery gases (a complex combination consisting
predominantly of hydrogen with small amounts of methane, ethane and
propane);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.7)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), refinery (a complex combination — obtained from
various petroleum refining operations — consisting of hydrogen and
hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of
C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>3</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.8)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), hydrotreated sour kerosine depentanizer stabilizer
off (a complex combination — obtained from the depentanizer
stabilization of hydrotreated kerosine — consisting predominantly of
hydrogen, methane, ethane and propane with small amounts of nitrogen,
hydrogen sulfide, carbon monoxide and hydrocarbons having carbon numbers
predominantly in the range of C<sub>4</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.9)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), crude oil fractionation off (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained from the fractionation of crude oil — consisting
of saturated aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly
in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.10)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), fluidized catalytic cracker fractionation off (a
complex combination — obtained from the fractionation of the overhead
products that result from a fluidized catalytic cracking process —
consisting of hydrogen, hydrogen sulfide, nitrogen and hydrocarbons
having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of C<sub>1</sub>
through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.11)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), heavy distillate hydrotreater desulfurization
stripper off (a complex combination — stripped from the liquid product
that results from a heavy distillate hydrotreater desulfurization
process — consisting of hydrogen, hydrogen sulfide and saturated
aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the range
of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.12)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), preflash tower off, crude distillation (a complex
combination — produced from the first tower used in the distillation of
crude oil — consisting of nitrogen and saturated aliphatic hydrocarbons
having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of C<sub>1</sub>
through C<sub>5</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.13)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), straight-run stabilizer off (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained from the fractionation of the liquid produced
from the first tower used in the distillation of crude oil — consisting
of saturated aliphatic hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly
in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.14)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
tail gas (petroleum), catalytic hydrodesulfurized naphtha separator (a
complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the catalytic
hydrodesulfurization of naphtha — consisting of hydrogen, methane,
ethane and propane);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.15)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), C<sub>3</sub>-C<sub>4</sub> (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation of products that result
from the cracking of crude oil — consisting of hydrocarbons having
carbon numbers in the range of C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>,
predominantly propane and propene, and boiling in the range of
approximately -51°C to -1°C);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.16)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), C<sub>3</sub>-C<sub>4</sub>, isobutane-rich (a
complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation of
saturated and unsaturated hydrocarbons having carbon numbers
predominantly in the range of C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>6</sub>,
predominantly butane and isobutane — consisting of saturated and
unsaturated hydrocarbons having carbon numbers in the range of
C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>4</sub>, predominantly isobutane);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.17)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
gases (petroleum), C<sub>4</sub>-rich (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained from the distillation of products that result
from a catalytic fractionation process — consisting of aliphatic
hydrocarbons having carbon numbers in the range of C<sub>3</sub> through
C<sub>5</sub>, predominantly C<sub>4</sub>); and
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.18)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
hydrocarbons, C<sub>1</sub>-C<sub>4</sub>, debutanizer fraction (a
complex combination of hydrocarbons — obtained from a debutanizing
process — having carbon numbers in the range of C<sub>1</sub> through
C<sub>4</sub>);
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.19)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
petroleum gases, liquefied (a complex combination of hydrocarbons —
obtained from the distillation of crude oil — consisting of hydrocarbons
having carbon numbers predominantly in the range of C<sub>3</sub>
through C<sub>7</sub> and boiling in the range of approximately -40°C to
80°C); and
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock0">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel"><strong>(z.20)</strong></span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
petroleum gases, liquefied, sweetened (a complex combination of
hydrocarbons — obtained by subjecting liquefied petroleum gases to a
sweetening process to convert mercaptans or to remove acidic impurities
— consisting of hydrocarbons having carbon numbers predominantly in the
range of C<sub>3</sub> through C<sub>7</sub> and boiling in the range of
approximately -40°C to 80°C).
</div>
</div></li>
</ul>
</figure></li>
